### ==============================================================
### EVENT - fires once MAD_AMBATOVY_TOTAL hits 100 (JV formed)
### Hook this into whichever effect field runs on successful
### early ACTIVATION, not TIMEOUT_EFFECT.
### ==============================================================
add_namespace = mad_ambatovy
add_namespace = mad_austral
add_namespace = mad_diplomacy
add_namespace = mad_plague
add_namespace = mad_apollo21

country_event = {
	id = mad_austral.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = MAD_Austral_offer
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = MAD_Austral_deal_falls_through }
			}
			complete_national_focus = MAD_Austral_deal_falls_through
		}
	}
}

### ==============================================================
### EVENT - fired from MAD_AMBATOVY_MISSION's remove_effect
### ==============================================================

country_event = {
	id = mad_ambatovy.1
	title = mad_ambatovy.1.t
	desc = {
		text = mad_ambatovy.1.d_dynatec
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_dynatec_invited }
	}
	desc = {
		text = mad_ambatovy.1.d_consortium
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_dynatec_invited } }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_mining_default

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_ambatovy.1.a # "Let Sherritt step in"
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_ambatovy.1 - Sherritt steps in"

		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_receiving_investment_duration_modifier = -0.10 tooltip = receiving_investment_duration_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_foreign_relations_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = foreign_influence_defense_modifier_tt }

		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = -0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_private_ownership
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_ambatovy.1.b # "Increase the state's stake"

		# Ambatovy's corporate-stake purchase remains a fixed $17bn cost.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -17.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = MAD_AMBATOVY_CORP value = -33.2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = MAD_AMBATOVY_STATE_FUNDS value = 33.2 }
		set_country_flag = MAD_ambatovy_state_bought_corporate_stake
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_dynatec_invited }
			set_country_flag = MAD_ambatovy_state_bought_dynatec
		}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_foreign_relations_modifier
			}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = 0.03 tooltip = foreign_influence_defense_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_infrastructure_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_ambatovy.1 - state buys out Dynatec"
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = 0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_public_ownership
		}
	}
}
add_namespace = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER
country_event = {
	id = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.1
	title = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.1.t
	desc = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept the offer
		name = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.a
		set_temp_variable = {
			treasury_change = {
				value = MAD.gdp_total
				multiply = -0.05 # Lufthansa purchase price: 5% of Madagascar GDP.
			}
		}
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = {
			int_investment_change = {
				value = MAD.gdp_total
				multiply = 0.015
			}
		}
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		# Lufthansa acceptance also increases German influence in Madagascar.
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = GER }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		MAD = {
			set_country_flag = MAD_GERMANS_ACCEPTED_LUFTHANSA
			country_event = { id = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.2 hours = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = { # Decline the offer
		name = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.b
		MAD = {
			set_country_flag = MAD_GERMANS_REJECTED_LUFTHANSA
			country_event = { id = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.3 hours = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = { # Fires on MAD - accept branch
	id = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.2
	title = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.2.t
	desc = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER_ACCEPT.a
		set_temp_variable = {
			treasury_change = {
				value = gdp_total
				multiply = 0.05 # Matching proceeds for the Lufthansa airline sale.
			}
		}
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_infrastructure_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_infrastructure_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.02 tooltip = infrastructure_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_receiving_investment_cost_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = receiving_investment_cost_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_offices_productivity = 0.05 tooltip = offices_productivity_tt }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

country_event = { # Fires on MAD - decline branch
	id = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.3
	title = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.3.t
	desc = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER.3.d

		name = MAD_LUFTHANSA_OFFER_DECLINE.a

		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

####TELMAEVENTS#####
add_namespace = mad_telma

country_event = {
	id = mad_telma.1
	title = mad_telma.1.t

	desc = {
		text = mad_telma.1.d_axian
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_TELMA_AXIAN_OWNED }
	}
	desc = {
		text = mad_telma.1.d_state
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_TELMA_STATE_OWNED }
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_telma.1.a # "Allow the new entrant"
		swap_ideas = {
		remove_idea = MAD_FIBRE_MONOPOLY
		add_idea = MAD_FIBRE_MONOPOLY_WEAKENED
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_mobile_competition_resolved
		add_stability = 0.02
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_telma.1 - competitor allowed"
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_telma.1.b # "Block the new entrant"
		swap_ideas = {
		remove_idea = MAD_FIBRE_MONOPOLY
		add_idea = MAD_FIBRE_MONOPOLY_PROTECTED_EARLY
		}
		newline = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 15 }
			change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		newline = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
			change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_mobile_competition_resolved
		increase_corruption = yes
		add_political_power = 25
		random_owned_controlled_state = {

			add_building_construction = {
						type = internet_station
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
						type = offices
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_telma.1 - competitor blocked"
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = -3 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
	}
}

add_namespace = mad_madarail

########################################################################
# mad_madarail.1  - terms of nationalisation (fired by MAD_MADARAIL_STATE_TAKEOVER)
########################################################################

country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.1
	title = mad_madarail.1.t
	desc = mad_madarail.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Option A: seize the network for nothing - cheap, messy, minor buffs
	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.1.a executed - seized Madarail without compensation"
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = -4 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		add_ideas = MAD_MADARAIL_OPERATOR_STATE_AUSTERE
		add_stability = -0.02
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_infrastructure_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = -0.10 tooltip = infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_receiving_investment_cost_modifier = 0.10 tooltip = receiving_investment_cost_modifier_tt }
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = 0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_public_ownership
		}
	}

	# Option B: take over on a stronger public footing - bigger buffs
	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.1.b executed - paid off Madarail's debts before taking over"
		add_ideas = MAD_MADARAIL_OPERATOR_STATE_FUNDED
		add_stability = 0.02
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_infrastructure_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = infrastructure_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		# Purchase compensation: match the initial Madarail privatisation value (8% of GDP).
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.08 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		# free infrastructure investment as part of the recapitalisation
		487 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		# Separately clear the concession's debts / recapitalise the operator.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = 0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_public_ownership
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# INVESTOR CYCLE
#
#   .2  hidden   start (fired by MAD_MADARAIL_OWNERSHIP_CHURN)
#   .3  popup    "The Bidders" - the player picks which remaining suitor takes the concession
#   .4  hidden   mission timeout: the suitor leaves; next bidders event, or forced exit after the third
#   .7  popup    STAY  (player completes the mission with confidence >= 75)
#   .8  popup    DESIST (fired by the "Let the Investors Walk" decision)
#   .9  popup    DESIST, forced (third suitor ran out its 150 days)
########################################################################

country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.2 - Madarail investor cycle starts"
		set_variable = { MAD_madarail_stage = 0 }
		set_variable = { MAD_madarail_confidence = 0 }
		country_event = mad_madarail.3
	}
}

# Pick a suitor. Each can be chosen once; on the third rotation only one is left.
country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.3
	title = mad_madarail.3.t
	desc = mad_madarail.3.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.3.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MADARAIL_COMAZAR_USED } }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.3.a executed - Comazar seated"
		MAD_madarail_seat_comazar = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 3 }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.3.b
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MADARAIL_VECTURIS_USED } }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.3.b executed - Vecturis seated"
		MAD_madarail_seat_vecturis = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 2 }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.3.c
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MADARAIL_HOLDING_USED } }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.3.c executed - Holding consortium seated"
		MAD_madarail_seat_holding = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# mission timeout: the suitor leaves without a commitment
country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.4
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MADARAIL_OUTCOME_DECIDED } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_madarail_stage < 3 } }
				log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.4 - the suitor leaves without committing, new bidders"
				MAD_madarail_clear_operators = yes
				MAD_madarail_clear_concessions = yes
				country_event = mad_madarail.3
			}
			else = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.4 - the third suitor ran out of time, investors desist"
				country_event = mad_madarail.9
			}
		}
	}
}

# STAY - the player completed the mission with enough confidence
country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.7
	title = mad_madarail.7.t
	desc = mad_madarail.7.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.7.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.7.a executed - Madarail investors stay"
		MAD_madarail_apply_stay_outcome = yes
	}
}

# DESIST - the government let them go
country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.8
	title = mad_madarail.8.t
	desc = mad_madarail.8.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.8.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.8.a executed - Madarail investors desist by decision"
		MAD_madarail_apply_desist_outcome = yes
	}
}

# DESIST - forced, all three suitors have come and gone
country_event = {
	id = mad_madarail.9
	title = mad_madarail.9.t
	desc = mad_madarail.9.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_madarail.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_madarail.9.a executed - Madarail investors desist, forced"
		MAD_madarail_apply_desist_outcome = yes
	}
}

# events/MAD_daewoo_events.txt
add_namespace = mad_daewoo

# Fired by MAD_DAEWOO_FALLOUT: the lease becomes public
country_event = {
	id = mad_daewoo.1
	title = mad_daewoo.1.t
	desc = mad_daewoo.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# Daewoo Fallout is already the trigger point for this event.
		# Schedule Viva directly; no daily polling is needed.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { MAD_AREMA_IN_POWER = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_SUCCESSOR_SELECTED }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RENEWAL }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RECONCILIATION }
				# Viva ban cannot happen under TGV, since Rajoelina leads TGV and owns viva.
				NOT = { MAD_TGV_IN_POWER = yes }
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_BANNED
					has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_RETURN_DENIED
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_PENDING }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_RESOLVED }
			}
			set_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_PENDING
			country_event = { id = mad_political.91 days = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_daewoo.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_daewoo.1 - the Daewoo lease is exposed"
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = KOR }
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1.5 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = KOR }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
	}
}

# The lease is signed off: it has been made to work
country_event = {
	id = mad_daewoo.2
	title = mad_daewoo.2.t
	desc = mad_daewoo.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_daewoo.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_daewoo.2 - Daewoo lease managed well"
		set_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_OUTCOME_WELL
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_DAEWOO_RURAL_COOP_DRAIN_TT
		MAD_rural_cooperation_calculate_monthly_change = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_FOOD_QUOTA }
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 2 }
			MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		}
	}
}

# The deadline passed and the lease is still a bad deal: the lease collapses and the government-failure focus is applied.
# The settlement and political penalties in MAD_DEAL_KEPT_FAILED are applied automatically.
country_event = {
	id = mad_daewoo.3
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_REVIEW_FAILED
	}

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_OUTCOME_FAILED
		MAD_daewoo_end_lease = yes
		add_timed_idea = { idea = MAD_DAEWOO_CANCELLED days = 1095 }
		MAD_autocomplete_forced_outcome_foci = yes
	}
}

# Six-month timer started when the Daewoo Deal focus completes.
# The historic story broke through press reporting that drew on the investor's own claims;
# available accounts do not establish that the Malagasy government deliberately leaked it.
country_event = {
	id = mad_daewoo.4
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = MAD_DAEWOO_DEAL
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = MAD_DAEWOO_FALLOUT }
			}
			country_event = mad_daewoo.5
		}
	}
}

# The only option completes the Daewoo Fallout focus; its completion reward then runs normally.
country_event = {
	id = mad_daewoo.5
	title = mad_daewoo.5.t
	desc = mad_daewoo.5.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_daewoo.5.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = MAD_DAEWOO_FALLOUT } }
			complete_national_focus = MAD_DAEWOO_FALLOUT
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# Madagascar drought and famine events
# Historical drought events affect drought and famine conditions.
# Population transfers are calculated only by the monthly mechanism from current state severity.
########################################################################

# events/MAD_drought_events.txt
add_namespace = mad_drought

# .2 monthly tick (re-fires itself every 30 days), .10/.11 random shocks,
# .12-.19 scheduled drought/food-crisis events, .20 drought over, .22 contained

country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		MAD_drought_monthly_tick = yes
		if = {
			limit = { MAD_drought_exists = yes }
			country_event = { id = mad_drought.2 days = 30 }
		}
	}

}

			hidden_effect = {
				# A swarm crosses the island, with the worst effects in the Grand Sud.
				# CNA measures reduce its base severity before it is applied.
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_swarm_base = 9 }

					limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_LOCUST_WATCH }
					set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_swarm_base = 5 }

			}
			MAD_drought_locust_swarm = yes

	desc = mad_drought.13.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	}

		name = mad_drought.13.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_WON } }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_drought.13 - 2015-16 El Nino drought"
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_EARLY_WARNING }
				268 = {
					set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 8 }
					MAD_drought_state_apply_change = yes
					set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 10 }
					MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				268 = {
					set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 13 }
					MAD_drought_state_apply_change = yes
					set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 13 }
					MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_shock_pressure = 0.40 }
				MAD_drought_add_warning_shock_pressure = yes
				add_to_variable = { MAD_drought_depop = 1 }
				MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# One event at most per monthly displacement tick. All positive transfers are staged first,
# and no state population is changed until the player confirms this option. Empty months do not queue this event.
country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.23
	title = mad_drought.displacement.t
	desc = mad_drought.23.d_default
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_drought.displacement.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.option_header_tt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_266 > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_266_positive_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_266 < -0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_266_negative_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_267 > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_267_positive_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_267 < -0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_267_negative_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_268 > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_268_positive_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_268 < -0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_268_negative_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_487 > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_487_positive_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_net_487 < -0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.net_487_negative_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_deaths_monthly > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.deaths_monthly_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_arrivals_COM > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.arrival_COM_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_arrivals_MOZ > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.arrival_MOZ_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_drought_report_arrivals_SAF > 0.5 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.displacement.arrival_SAF_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			MAD_drought_commit_displacement_266 = yes
			MAD_drought_commit_displacement_267 = yes
			MAD_drought_commit_displacement_268 = yes
			MAD_drought_commit_displacement_487 = yes
			MAD_drought_update_displacement_overload = yes
			clr_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_DISPLACEMENT_REPORT_PENDING
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# THE DROUGHT MAP - events that hit STATES, not the country
########################################################################
# .30 the rains fail in one region   .31 a cyclone (rain, but the wrong kind)   .32 a state tips into famine
# .33 the wells run dry in one state
#
# These are what make the map move unevenly: a countrywide number would never show you that Androy is finished
# while Analamanga is fine.

# Select the target in the monthly launcher before this popup is displayed. The same saved state
# supplies the rendered name and receives all of the event's consequences.
country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.30
	title = mad_drought.30.t
	desc = mad_drought.30.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { MAD_drought_shocks_allowed = yes }

	option = {
		name = mad_drought.30.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_WON } }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_drought.30 - rains fail in the selected state"
			event_target:MAD_drought_event_state = {
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 9 }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 5 }
				MAD_drought_state_apply_shock = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_shock_pressure = 0.25 }
				MAD_drought_add_warning_shock_pressure = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
# A cyclone causes destructive runoff and damages crops, soil and infrastructure in the selected coastal state.
country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.31
	title = mad_drought.31.t
	desc = mad_drought.31.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { MAD_drought_shocks_allowed = yes }

	option = {
		name = mad_drought.31.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_WON } }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_drought.31 - cyclone in the selected coastal state"
			event_target:MAD_drought_event_state = {
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 7 }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 10 }
				MAD_drought_state_apply_shock = yes
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure > 0 }
					damage_building = {
						type = infrastructure
						damage = 1
					}
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
			}
			add_stability = -0.01
		}
	}
}
# A qualifying state's food insecurity has crossed into severe famine conditions.
country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.32
	title = mad_drought.32.t
	desc = mad_drought.32.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { MAD_drought_shocks_allowed = yes }

	option = {
		name = mad_drought.32.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_WON } }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_drought.32 - famine in the selected state"
			event_target:MAD_drought_event_state = {
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 18 }
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_EARLY_WARNING } }
					multiply_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 0.75 }
				}
				MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
			}
			add_stability = -0.02
			add_war_support = -0.01
		}
	}
}
# The selected state's well flag is cleared when its hand pumps no longer provide dependable water.
country_event = {
	id = mad_drought.33
	title = mad_drought.33.t
	desc = mad_drought.33.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { MAD_drought_shocks_allowed = yes }

	option = {
		name = mad_drought.33.a
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_WON } }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_drought.33 - wells run dry in the selected state"
			event_target:MAD_drought_event_state = {
				hidden_effect = {
					clr_state_flag = MAD_STATE_WELLS
				}
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 8 }
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DROUGHT_EARLY_WARNING } }
					multiply_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_change = 0.75 }
				}
				MAD_drought_state_apply_change = yes
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { MAD_state_drought_exposure = 0.15 }
					clamp_variable = { var = MAD_state_drought_exposure min = 0.4 max = 2 }
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = mad_drought.33_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# FOREIGN INVESTMENT PURCHASE OFFERS
########################################################################
# The foreign buyer pays 75% of the proposed investment from its own treasury.
# Madagascar receives the full investment value only after the buyer accepts.

add_namespace = mad_foreign_investment

country_event = {
	id = mad_foreign_investment.1
	title = mad_foreign_investment.1.t
	desc = mad_foreign_investment.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_foreign_investment.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { add = 60 check_variable = { treasury > 7.499 } }
			modifier = { add = 10 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 20 } }
			modifier = { add = 30 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 79 } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -7.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 10 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 10 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = KOR }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		MAD = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.50 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			country_event = { id = mad_foreign_investment.2 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_foreign_investment.1.b
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_foreign_investment.3 days = 1 } }
	}
}

########################################################################
# QMM LAGOON POLLUTION SCANDAL
#
# Scheduled 100 days after MAD_EHOALA_PORT completes. Both outcomes permanently
# resolve the scandal and unlock the Bemolanga oil-sands and Ranobe mineral-sands projects.
########################################################################

add_namespace = mad_qmm

country_event = {
	id = mad_qmm.1
	title = mad_qmm.1.t
	desc = mad_qmm.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_qmm.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_qmm.1.a - QMM scandal covered up"
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_QMM_COVERUP_COOP_TT
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = -25 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes

		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		}
		increase_corruption = yes
		set_temp_variable = { corp_change = 5 }
		modify_corporate_tax_rate_effect = yes
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_QMM_LAGOON_COVER_UP
			days = 365
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_QMM_LAGOON_SCANDAL_RESOLVED
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = -0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_private_ownership
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_qmm.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_qmm.1.b - QMM scandal investigated and cleaned up"
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_QMM_CLEANUP_COOP_TT
		# Transparent remediation restores part, but not all, of the trust lost to the scandal.
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 5 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes

		decrease_corruption = yes
		set_temp_variable = { corp_change = -5 }
		modify_corporate_tax_rate_effect = yes
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_QMM_LAGOON_CLEANUP
			days = 365
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_QMM_LAGOON_SCANDAL_RESOLVED
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = 0.075
			tooltip_side = MAD_public_ownership
		}
	}
}

########################################################################
# VANILLA EXPORT MARKET CYCLE
#
# Started by MAD_VANILLA_BOOM_BUST_CYCLE.
# One event handles both the boom and bust states. The player gets a small
# response decision at each shock, so the branch plays as:
#
#   export policy -> market shock -> player response -> next shock
#
# MAD_vanilla_shock_state:
#    0   = no previous temporary shock
#   11   = boom / reinvest
#   12   = boom / bank profits
#  -11   = bust / support growers
#  -12   = bust / let market correct
########################################################################

add_namespace = mad_vanilla

country_event = {
	id = mad_vanilla.1
	title = mad_vanilla.1.t
	desc = {
		text = mad_vanilla.1.d_boom
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM }
	}
	desc = {
		text = mad_vanilla.1.d_bust
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_VOLATILE_MARKET }

	immediate = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_vanilla.1 - vanilla market shock"

		# Remove the previous temporary market shock before applying the next one.
		# Stability and political-power changes from the previous response are
		# deliberately one-off and are not reversed.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 11 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = -0.10 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = -0.04 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 12 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
			}
			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = -0.06 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = -0.02 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = -0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -11 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = 0.04 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = 0.01 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = 0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -12 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = 0.08 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = 0.03 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
			}

			add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = 0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		}

		clr_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM
		clr_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST

		# Calculate this market turn from inflation at the time the event fires.
		# MD stores inflation_rate_var as a fraction: 0.15 = 15%.
		# At 15% inflation or higher, a bust is more likely (65%).
		# Below 15%, a boom is more likely (55%).
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = inflation_rate_var
					NOT = { check_variable = { inflation_rate_var < 0.15 } }
				}
				random_list = {
					65 = {
						set_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST
						set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -1 }
					}
					35 = {
						set_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM
						set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					55 = {
						set_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM
						set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 1 }
					}
					45 = {
						set_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST
						set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# The exact response options overwrite 1/-1 with 11/12/-11/-12,
		# preserving which temporary package must be reversed next cycle.

	}

	option = {
		name = mad_vanilla.1.a
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_vanilla.1.a - reinvest in the vanilla boom"

		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = 0.10 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = 0.04 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_political_power = 10
		set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 11 }

		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = mad_vanilla.1 days = 600 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_vanilla.1.b
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BOOM }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_vanilla.1.b - bank the vanilla profits"

		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = 0.06 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = 0.02 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = 0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = 0.04
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = 12 }

		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = mad_vanilla.1 days = 600 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_vanilla.1.c
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST
			check_variable = { treasury > 1.999 }
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_vanilla.1.c - support vanilla growers"

		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = -0.04 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = -0.01 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = -0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = 0.01
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -11 }

		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = mad_vanilla.1 days = 600 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_vanilla.1.d
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_VANILLA_PRICE_BUST }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_vanilla.1.d - let the vanilla market correct itself"

		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_international_market_income_modifier = -0.08 tooltip = international_market_income_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_return_on_investment_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = return_on_investment_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier
		}

		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_expected_welfare_modifier = -0.25 tooltip = expected_welfare_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_political_power = -25
		set_variable = { MAD_vanilla_shock_state = -12 }

		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = mad_vanilla.1 days = 600 }
		}
	}
}

#- add to MAD_events.txt
# ============================================================================
add_namespace = mad_jirama

country_event = {
	id = mad_jirama.1
	title = mad_jirama.1.t
	desc = mad_jirama.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_jirama.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_jirama.1.a - cost-reflective tariffs"
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = -4 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_JIRAMA_TARIFF_DISCIPLINE
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_power_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_pop_energy_use_multiplier = -0.03 tooltip = pop_energy_use_multiplier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_energy_use_modifier_civs = -0.01 tooltip = energy_use_modifier_civs_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_fossil_pp_fuel_consumption_modifier = -0.02 tooltip = fossil_pp_fuel_consumption_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.03 tooltip = population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = -0.03
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
		change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -10 }
		change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -2 }
		change_landowners_opinion = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_jirama.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_jirama.1.b - state recapitalisation"
		set_country_flag = MAD_JIRAMA_STATE_BACKSTOP
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.013 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_power_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_energy_gain_multiplier = 0.03 tooltip = energy_gain_multiplier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_renewable_energy_gain_multiplier = 0.02 tooltip = renewable_energy_gain_multiplier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_internal_investments_money_cost_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = internal_investments_money_cost_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = 0.02
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
		change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
		change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
		change_landowners_opinion = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_jirama.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_jirama.1.c - competitive private contracts"
		set_country_flag = MAD_JIRAMA_PRIVATE_CONTRACTS
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_power_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_production_speed_renewable_energy_infra_factor = 0.05 tooltip = production_speed_renewable_energy_infra_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_power_renewable_infra_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = renewable_infra_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_investment_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_receiving_investment_cost_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = receiving_investment_cost_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_receiving_investment_duration_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = receiving_investment_duration_modifier_tt }
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
			MODIFIER = MAD_foreign_relations_modifier
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_foreign_influence_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = foreign_influence_modifier_tt }
		add_stability = -0.01
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 8 }

# common/events/MAD_dahalo_events.txt
# Escalation events for the permanent Dahalo system.

add_namespace = mad_dahalo

# Maximum Dahalo strength after the Permit Arms route: open revolt in the Grand Sud.
country_event = {
	id = mad_dahalo.1
	title = mad_dahalo.1.t
	desc = mad_dahalo.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_dahalo.1.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_DAHALO_CIVIL_WAR_START_TT
		hidden_effect = {
			MAD_dahalo_prepare_for_civil_war = yes
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = neutrality
				# Preserve the original government's party; this parameter would change Madagascar's ruling party.
				keep_political_leader = yes
				size = 0.55
				army_ratio = 0.65
				navy_ratio = 0
				air_ratio = 0
				capital = 268
				states = { 268 }

				# Effects inside start_civil_war are scoped to the breakaway country.
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = neutrality
					long_name = MAD_DAHALO_REBEL_GOVERNMENT
					name = MAD_DAHALO_REBEL_GOVERNMENT
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
					elections_allowed = no
				}
				# Madagascar party 13 is the Dahalo Council (Neutral Autocracy).
				# Reuse its existing leader mapping so the breakaway is led by Remenabila.
				set_variable = { ruling_party = 13 }
				set_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
				set_leader_MAD = yes
			}
		}
	}}

country_event = {
	id = mad_dahalo.10
	title = mad_dahalo.10.t
	desc = mad_dahalo.10.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_dahalo.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_dahalo.10.a - emergency restocking"
		268 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 5 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_to_variable = { MAD_dahalo_threat = 5 tooltip = MAD_DAHALO_THREAT_PLUS_5_TT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity > -0.039 }
					check_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax > -0.029 }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_agriculture_modifier
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity > -0.039 } }
				add_to_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity = -0.01 tooltip = agricolture_productivity_modifier_tt }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax > -0.029 } }
				add_to_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax = -0.01 tooltip = agriculture_district_income_tax_modifier_tt }
			}
		}
		add_stability = -0.02

		MAD_dahalo_update_modifier = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_dahalo.10.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_dahalo.10.b - accept the loss"
		268 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = 10 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { MAD_dahalo_threat = 8 tooltip = MAD_DAHALO_THREAT_PLUS_8_TT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity > -0.039 }
					check_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax > -0.029 }
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt
				MODIFIER = MAD_agriculture_modifier
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity > -0.039 } }
				add_to_variable = { MAD_agri_productivity = -0.05 tooltip = agricolture_productivity_modifier_tt }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax > -0.029 } }
				add_to_variable = { MAD_agri_income_tax = -0.02 tooltip = agriculture_district_income_tax_modifier_tt }
			}
		}
		add_stability = -0.03
		MAD_dahalo_update_modifier = yes

	}
}

	desc = { text = mad_dahalo.20.d_failure trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_MAZAVA_CONTESTED } }
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_MAZAVA_SUCCESS }
		name = mad_dahalo.20.a
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_MAZAVA_CONTESTED }
		name = mad_dahalo.20.b
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Royal Crown Question
# Triggered only by MAD_POL_ROY_CROWN_QUESTION.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.1
	title = mad_political.1.t
	desc = mad_political.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_ROYAL_CROWN_QUESTION_PENDING
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = MAD_ROYAL_CROWN_QUESTION_PENDING
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.1.a
		set_country_flag = MAD_RABARIOELINA_ABSOLUTE
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TAMIMA_COUNCIL
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		increase_centralization = yes

		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 23 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 15 }
		ban_party_scripted_call = yes
		recalculate_party = yes


	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.1.b
		set_country_flag = MAD_TAMIMA_COUNCIL
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RABARIOELINA_ABSOLUTE
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		decrease_centralization = yes

		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
		ban_party_scripted_call = yes
		recalculate_party = yes


	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Daewoo political fallout
# The Daewoo Logistics land deal became a wider political grievance in late
# 2008/early 2009. Cancellation itself does not cause a generic election:
# the player decides how the resulting legitimacy crisis is handled.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.94
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_BROKEN
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_REVIEW_FAILED
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_OUTCOME_FAILED
		}
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FALLOUT_DONE }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_elections = yes }
			country_event = { id = mad_political.2 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = mad_political.93 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2a. Daewoo political fallout under a government without elections
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.93
	title = mad_political.93.t
	desc = mad_political.93.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_elections = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_BROKEN
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_REVIEW_FAILED
			has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_OUTCOME_FAILED
		}
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FALLOUT_DONE }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FALLOUT_DONE
		MAD_UNLOCK_POL_CONTENT_2009_2025 = yes

		# Find the largest eligible opposition party.
		# MD's party system stores party support in party_pop_array^index.
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY_POPULARITY = -1 }

		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = 24
			compare = less_than
			value = p
			# is_party_banned_trigger reads party_index, so set it as an effect before evaluating limit.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = p }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { ruling_party = p } }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { gov_coalition_array = p } }
					NOT = { is_party_banned_trigger = yes }
					check_variable = { party_pop_array^p > 0 }
					check_variable = { party_pop_array^p > MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY_POPULARITY }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY = p }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY_POPULARITY = party_pop_array^p }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.93.a
		trigger = {
					has_civil_war = no
				NOT = { check_variable = { MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY < 0 } }
			}

		custom_effect_tooltip = mad_political.93.a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Reproduce MD's party-specific revolt/civil-war pattern, using the
			# largest eligible opposition party found above instead of a GUI slot.
			set_temp_variable = { v = MAD_DAEWOO_NEXT_PARTY }
			start_civil_war_with_no_leader_change = yes
	
			random_other_country = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = PREV
					NOT = { TAG = PREV }
				}
				civil_war_replication_effect = yes
			}
	
			set_variable = { party_pop_array^v = 0.7 }
			startup_politics = yes
			# Select the party and let MD derive its ideology and dispatch MAD's party leader.
			set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = v }
			set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
			MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
			change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.93.b

		# Preserve the incumbent government's ideology when restoring elections.
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = nationalist }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = yes }
		}
		else = {
			set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = yes }
		}
		country_event = MD4_election_campaign.0
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.2
	title = mad_political.2.t
	desc = mad_political.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_elections = yes
		MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FAILURE = yes
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FALLOUT_DONE }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = MAD_DAEWOO_POLITICAL_FALLOUT_DONE
		MAD_UNLOCK_POL_CONTENT_2009_2025 = yes
	}

	# ============================================================
	# A — Keep the civilian government / turn cancellation into
	#     a sovereignty victory
	# ============================================================
	option = {
		name = mad_political.2.a
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.05 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = MAD_economy_modifier }
		add_to_variable = { MAD_economy_population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = -0.08 tooltip = population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier_tt }

		# The incumbent government is damaged by the scandal,
		# while the opposition benefits.
		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = -0.025 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		add_stability = -0.15

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.06 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.06 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		recalculate_party = yes
	}

	# ============================================================
	# B — Military takeover
	# ============================================================
	option = {
		name = mad_political.2.b


		add_stability = -0.05

		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		# Military party takes power.
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		# The refoundation choice follows only after military rule is established.
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_REFOUNDATION_PENDING
		country_event = { id = mad_political.3 days = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_2009_INTERNATIONAL_ISOLATION
			days = 1460
		}

		recalculate_party = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_DIRECTORATE_PENDING
		country_event = { id = MAD_military_refoundation.16 days = 2 }
	}

	# ============================================================
	# C — TGV-led transitional government
	# ============================================================
	option = {
		name = mad_political.2.c

		add_stability = -0.03
		increase_corruption = yes

		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = -0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		# TGV becomes the actual ruling MD party.
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 0 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes

		# TGV gets the political benefit.
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		# Military gains some influence from having forced the crisis,
		# but less than under direct military rule.
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_2009_INTERNATIONAL_ISOLATION
			days = 1095
		}

		recalculate_party = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_2009_TRANSITIONAL_ROUTE
		country_event = { id = MAD_military_refoundation.8 days = 1 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Military refoundation Refoundation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.3
	title = mad_political.3.t
	desc = mad_political.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_MIL_REFOUNDATION_PENDING
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_REFOUNDATION_PENDING
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.3.a # Professionalise
		remove_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GUARDIAN
		remove_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GOVERNMENT
		add_ideas = MAD_MIL_PROFESSIONAL_COMMAND
		set_politics = { elections_allowed = yes }
		decrease_corruption = yes
		add_political_power = 35
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.04 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.3.b # Constitutional guardian
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_REFOUNDATION_GUARDIAN
		remove_ideas = MAD_MIL_PROFESSIONAL_COMMAND
		remove_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GOVERNMENT
		add_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GUARDIAN
		set_politics = { elections_allowed = yes }
		add_political_power = 25

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.03 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.3.c # Final arbiter
		remove_ideas = MAD_MIL_PROFESSIONAL_COMMAND
		remove_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GUARDIAN
		add_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GOVERNMENT
		add_political_power = 25

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.07 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.07 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.03 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}

	id = mad_political.5

	desc = mad_political.5.d

		add_political_power = 25
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }

	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.5.c

		decrease_corruption = yes
		add_political_power = 20
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.04 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. The Tiko Question
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.6
	title = mad_political.6.t
	desc = mad_political.6.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_TIM_TIKO_PENDING
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TIM_TIKO_PENDING
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.6.a
		add_ideas = MAD_TIM_TIKO_FIREWALL
		set_country_flag = MAD_TIM_TIKO_FIREWALL
		decrease_corruption = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
		change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
		change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.04 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = -0.05
			tooltip_side = MAD_private_ownership
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.6.b
		add_ideas = MAD_TIM_TIKO_NATIONAL_CHAMPION
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
		change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
		change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.04 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
		add_power_balance_value = {
			id = MAD_economy_ownership_bop
			value = -0.10
			tooltip_side = MAD_private_ownership
		}
	}


}

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.8
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# Restore the current government's earned programme ideas before applying party-conditioned constitutional policy.

		MAD_rural_governance_policy_sync = yes
		MAD_rural_constitution_initialize = yes
	}

# Ratsiraka’s first loss and the follow-up exile choice.
# Successor-led AREMA routes suppress this chain and its later Viva interview.

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.90
	title = mad_political.90.t
	desc = mad_political.90.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_SUCCESSOR_SELECTED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RENEWAL }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RECONCILIATION }
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.90.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = mad_political.90.a_tt
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_political.90.a selected"
		# Keep Marcel Ranjeva with the original country: this becomes Ratsiraka's side of the civil war.
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EVENT_QUEUED
			clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_FIRST_LOSS_PENDING
			clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_COALITION_TRACKING
			clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_COALITION_TIMED_OUT
			set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_FIRST_LOSS_DONE
			set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILE_RESOLVED
			set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CIVIL_WAR_STARTED
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_FORMATION_STABLE_DAYS = 0 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
			# Cache the elected government's standard HOI4 ideology shares BEFORE the civil war.
			# MD's replication helper runs startup_politics again, which can overwrite these shares.
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_DEMOCRATIC = { value = party_popularity@democratic multiply = 100 } }
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_COMMUNISM = { value = party_popularity@communism multiply = 100 } }
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NEUTRALITY = { value = party_popularity@neutrality multiply = 100 } }
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_FASCISM = { value = party_popularity@fascism multiply = 100 } }
			set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NATIONALIST = { value = party_popularity@nationalist multiply = 100 } }
			set_temp_variable = {
				MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_TOTAL_ADJUSTMENT = {
					value = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_DEMOCRATIC
					add = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_COMMUNISM
					add = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NEUTRALITY
					add = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_FASCISM
					add = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NATIONALIST
					subtract = 100
					multiply = -1
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NATIONALIST = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_TOTAL_ADJUSTMENT }

			# Create the civil war and copy the elected government.
			set_country_flag = no_leader_change

			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = ROOT
				ruling_party = ROOT
				keep_political_leader = no
				size = 0.5
				states = all
				states_filter = {
					is_capital = no
				}
			}

			clr_country_flag = no_leader_change

			random_other_country = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = PREV
					NOT = { TAG = PREV }
				}
				civil_war_replication_effect = yes
				MAD_RESTORE_CIVIL_WAR_OPPONENT_LEADER = yes

				# Reapply the pre-war standard ideology split after MD's politics startup.
				# The party arrays/ruling party remain those copied by civil_war_replication_effect.
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_DEMOCRATIC = PREV.MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_DEMOCRATIC }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_COMMUNISM = PREV.MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_COMMUNISM }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_NEUTRALITY = PREV.MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NEUTRALITY }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_FASCISM = PREV.MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_FASCISM }
				set_temp_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_NATIONALIST = PREV.MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NATIONALIST }
				set_popularities = {
					democratic = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_DEMOCRATIC
					communism = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_COMMUNISM
					neutrality = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_NEUTRALITY
					fascism = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_FASCISM
					nationalist = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_COPY_NATIONALIST
				}
			}

			# These one-use cached values have now been copied to the breakaway country.
			clear_variable = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_DEMOCRATIC
			clear_variable = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_COMMUNISM
			clear_variable = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NEUTRALITY
			clear_variable = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_FASCISM
			clear_variable = MAD_RATSIRAKA_CW_PREWAR_NATIONALIST

			# Turn the original, player-controlled Madagascar into AREMA's side.
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.5
			}
			start_politics_input = yes
			set_variable = { party_pop_array^5 = 0.7 }
			startup_politics = yes
			set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
			MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
			change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		}
		MAD_UNLOCK_POL_CONTENT_2002_2009 = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.90.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_political.90.b selected"
		# Ratsiraka chooses exile instead of civil war; retire Ranjeva when Ratsiraka loses power.
		if = { limit = { has_character = MAD_marcel_ranjeva } retire_character = MAD_marcel_ranjeva }
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EVENT_QUEUED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_FIRST_LOSS_PENDING
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_COALITION_TRACKING
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_COALITION_TIMED_OUT
		set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_FIRST_LOSS_DONE
		set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILE_EVENT_PENDING
		set_variable = { MAD_RATSIRAKA_FORMATION_STABLE_DAYS = 0 }
		country_event = { id = mad_ratsiraka.2 days = 1 }
		MAD_UNLOCK_POL_CONTENT_2002_2009 = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_ratsiraka.2
	title = mad_political.98.t
	desc = mad_political.98.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILE_EVENT_PENDING
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_SUCCESSOR_SELECTED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RENEWAL }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RECONCILIATION }
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.98.a
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILE_EVENT_PENDING
		set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILE_RESOLVED
		set_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_EXILED
		set_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_BANNED
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 5 }
		ban_party_scripted_call = yes
		MAD_UNLOCK_POL_CONTENT_2002_2009 = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.91
	title = mad_political.91.t
	desc = mad_political.91.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_radio
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD
		NOT = { MAD_AREMA_IN_POWER = yes }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_SUCCESSOR_SELECTED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RENEWAL }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_RECONCILIATION }
		has_completed_focus = MAD_DAEWOO_FALLOUT
		OR = {
			# Ratsiraka is still in self-selected exile because AREMA remains banned.
			has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_BANNED
			# Or AREMA was unbanned, but the return decision was refused.
			has_country_flag = MAD_RATSIRAKA_RETURN_DENIED
		}
		has_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_PENDING
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_RESOLVED }
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_PENDING
		set_country_flag = MAD_VIVA_INTERVIEW_RESOLVED

	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.91.a

		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.08 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMED } }
			# Defer TGV's +10% support until the formation event so the party remains absent
			# from MD's compact Politics View during the 15-day formation delay.
			set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_INITIAL_SUPPORT_PENDING
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
		}

		add_stability = -0.02
		add_war_support = -0.02
		add_political_power = 150

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMED } }
			set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMATION_PENDING
			country_event = { id = mad_political.95 days = 2 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.91.b

		# If AREMA is still banned, allowing the channel also restores AREMA
		# through MD's normal individual-party unban system. This happens after
		# Daewoo Fallout, so the Ratsiraka-return event is deliberately not reopened.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_BANNED }
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 5 }
			unban_party_scripted_call = yes
			clr_country_flag = MAD_AREMA_BANNED
		}

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.04 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		add_stability = 0.01
		add_political_power = -150
		

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMED } }
			set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMATION_PENDING
			country_event = { id = mad_political.95 days = 2 }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TGV formation
# .95 - delayed response after mad_political.91
# .96 - December 2007 formation event
# Both use the same MAD_TGV_FORMED latch, so only one formation route can fire.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.95
	title = mad_political.95.t
	desc = mad_political.95.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD
		has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMATION_PENDING
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMED }
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMATION_PENDING
		set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_FORMED
		set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_BRANCH_UNLOCKED

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_INITIAL_SUPPORT_PENDING }
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
			clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_INITIAL_SUPPORT_PENDING
		}

		# TGV is now formally established; add its party leader.

		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_political.95.a

		create_country_leader = {
			name = "Andry Rajoelina"
			picture = "Andry_Rajoelina.dds"
			expire = "2050.1.1"
			ideology = Nat_Populism
			traits = {
				nationalist_Nat_Populism
				career_politician
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}


country_event = {
	id = mad_political.99
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD
		tag = MAD
		date > 2023.12.31
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_ISSUED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_FAILED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED }
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { MAD_MIL_IN_POWER = yes }
				has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
				has_civil_war = no
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF } }
					AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT } }
				}
			}
			# Remove pre-existing generic MD protest state before the pressure year starts.
			# The cleanup cooldown is cleared so MD's shared monthly strength drift continues.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_MD_PROTESTS_INITIALISED } }
				remove_mission = MD_incoming_protest_movement
				remove_mission = MD_incoming_revolt
				remove_mission = MD_zero_strength_recovery
				MD_cleanup_protest_revolt = yes
				clr_country_flag = MD_protest_cooldown
				set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_MD_PROTESTS_INITIALISED
			}
			if = {
				limit = { MAD_TGV_IN_POWER = yes }
				set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_UNDER_TGV
				add_ideas = MAD_SPLIT_LEADERSHIP
			}
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_ISSUED
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_ACTIVE
			add_timed_idea = { idea = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_PRESSURE days = 365 }
			activate_mission = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_PRESSURE_MISSION
			# From this point on, only MAD's generic MD movement chain is suppressed.
			# The shared monthly protest_strength calculation remains active. 
			set_country_flag = disabled_protests_system
			country_event = { id = mad_political.100 days = 1 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = mad_political.99 days = 365 } }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.102
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD
		tag = MAD
		date > 2019.12.31
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_THRESHOLD_MET
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_STARTED }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED }
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { MAD_MIL_IN_POWER = yes }
				has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
				has_civil_war = no
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF } }
					AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT } }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF }
				set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
				clr_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_CAPSAT
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_CAPSAT
				clr_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
			}
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_STARTED
			# The modern crisis supersedes the five-year legacy CAPSAT confrontation.
			set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_TIMER_CANCELLED
			clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_DEFERRED
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_ACTIVE
			country_event = { id = mad_political.103 days = 1 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = mad_political.102 days = 365 } }
	}
}

	option = {

				country_event = { id = MAD_military_command.5 days = 1 }
			}
			country_event = { id = mad_political.110 days = 1 }
		}
		else = { country_event = { id = mad_political.104 days = 14 } }
	}

	}

		name = mad_political.103.c
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRACKDOWN
		add_stability = -0.06
		add_to_variable = { protest_radicalisation = 2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = protest_radicalisation min = 0 max = 10 }
		add_to_variable = { protest_strength = 5 }
		clamp_variable = { var = protest_strength min = 0 max = 100 }
		country_event = { id = mad_political.104 days = 14 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.106
	title = mad_political.106.t
	desc = mad_political.106.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_ACTIVE
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_MILITARY_DEFECTED
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED }
		has_civil_war = no
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_political.106.a
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PARLIAMENTARY_CHALLENGE_ACCEPTED
		add_stability = -0.02
