	}
	option = {
		name = mad_political.106.b
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_DISSOLUTION_ATTEMPT
		add_stability = -0.06
		country_event = { id = mad_political.107 days = 7 }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_political.106.c
		trigger = {
			owns_state = 267
			267 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			OR = {
				487 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				266 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				268 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = mad_political.106.c_tt
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CIVILIAN_RESISTANCE
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CIVIL_WAR_STARTED
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_ACTIVE
		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
		# The civil-war branch is a terminal conclusion of the modern crisis.
		clr_country_flag = disabled_protests_system
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_DEFERRED
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF }
			set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_GENERAL_STAFF_PARTY_LEADER = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_CAPSAT_PARTY_LEADER = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 487 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
			set_capital = { state = 487 remember_old_capital = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 266 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
			set_capital = { state = 266 remember_old_capital = no }
		}
		else = { set_capital = { state = 268 remember_old_capital = no } }
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CIVIL_WAR_CAPITAL_MOVED
		add_stability = -0.12
		start_civil_war = {
			ideology = nationalist
			keep_political_leader = yes
			size = 0.45
			army_ratio = 0.70
			navy_ratio = 0
			air_ratio = 0
			capital = 267
			states = { 267 }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }
			set_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
			set_variable = { party_pop_array^22 = 1 }
			set_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
			# The crisis has concluded at the civil-war split; do not carry over
			# its temporary suppression of the generic MD protest movement.
			clr_country_flag = disabled_protests_system
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_MD_PROTESTS_INITIALISED
			# If this side later inherits the MAD tag after winning, the monthly
			# scheduler must recognise the crisis as already resolved.
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CIVILIAN_RESISTANCE
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CIVIL_WAR_STARTED
			set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED
			set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
			set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF } }
				set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
				clr_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
				clr_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
			}
# Explicitly inherit the selected live command-system values from the original government.
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_COMMAND_POWER_GAIN_MULT = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_COMMAND_POWER_GAIN_MULT }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_TERRAIN_PENALTY_REDUCTION = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_TERRAIN_PENALTY_REDUCTION }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ATTACK_FACTOR = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ATTACK_FACTOR }

			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_FACTOR = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_FACTOR }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_TO_OTHERS = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_TO_OTHERS }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_REQUIRED_GARRISON_FACTOR = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_REQUIRED_GARRISON_FACTOR }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_LAND_REINFORCE_RATE = PREV.MAD_ARMY_CMD_LAND_REINFORCE_RATE }

country_event = {
	id = mad_political.108
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD tag = MAD
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_STARTED
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_ACTIVE
		has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_MILITARY_DEFECTED
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED }
		has_civil_war = no
		OR = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_TRANSITION_COUNCIL has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_DIRECT_MILITARY_RULE }
		OR = {
			AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_CAPSAT } }
			AND = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_CAPSAT NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF } }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF }
			set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_GENERAL_STAFF_PARTY_LEADER = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_CAPSAT_PARTY_LEADER = yes
		}
		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
		add_ideas = MAD_POL_MILITARY_GOVERNMENT
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_TRANSITION_COUNCIL }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = MAD_MODERN_TRANSITIONAL_COUNCIL days = 180 }
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
		# The military transfer concludes the modern crisis; suppression was temporary.
		clr_country_flag = disabled_protests_system
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_DEFERRED
		set_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_ACTIVE
		country_event = { id = mad_political.110 days = 1 }
	}
}


# Resolve the Split Leadership idea only when the pressure campaign or the full crisis reaches an endpoint.
# Rajoelina's departure and the TGV ban are exclusive to a TGV-origin crisis ending in direct military administration.
country_event = {
	id = mad_political.110
	title = mad_political.110.t
	desc = {
		text = mad_political.110.d_military
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_DIRECT_MILITARY_RULE
				has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_UNDER_TGV
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = mad_political.110.d_catalyst_failed
		trigger = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_FAILED }
	}
	desc = {
		text = mad_political.110.d_other
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_FAILED
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_DIRECT_MILITARY_RULE
						has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_UNDER_TGV
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = MAD
		tag = MAD
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED
			has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_FAILED
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_political.110.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Split Leadership ends with the protest-pressure campaign or modern crisis"
		remove_ideas = MAD_SPLIT_LEADERSHIP
		# Clear this legacy marker for existing saves; Richard is not appointed or retired by this event.
		clr_country_flag = MAD_RICHARD_INTERIM_OFFICE
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_DIRECT_MILITARY_RULE
				has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_UNDER_TGV
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Andry Rajoelina flees to Dubai after direct military administration is established"
			set_country_flag = MAD_RAJOELINA_FLED_DUBAI
			set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_BANNED
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 20 }
			ban_party_scripted_call = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
		}
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# MADAGASCAR ARMY — command and procurement events
# ============================================================================
add_namespace = MAD_military_command
add_namespace = MAD_military_procurement
add_namespace = MAD_military_industry

country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_command.1
	title = MAD_military_command.1.t
	desc = MAD_military_command.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_command.1.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF }
				}
				has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
					has_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
					has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF }
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
					has_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
					has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Command settlement - CAPSAT leadership"
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM_ACTIVATED_TT
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED } }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_COMMAND_POWER_GAIN_MULT = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_TERRAIN_PENALTY_REDUCTION = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ATTACK_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ORG_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_TRAINING_TIME_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_FOREIGN_INFLUENCE_DEFENSE_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_TO_OTHERS = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_REQUIRED_GARRISON_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_LAND_REINFORCE_RATE = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ROOT_OUT_RESISTANCE_EFFECTIVENESS_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_SPECIAL_FORCES_CAP_FLAT = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_CORE_DEFENCE_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_EXPERIENCE_GAIN_ARMY_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PERSONNEL_COST_MULTIPLIER_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_MILITARY_SPENDING_COST_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_RESEARCH_SPEED_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_MILITARY_LEADER_COST_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_EQUIPMENT_COST_MULTIPLIER_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PRODUCTION_SPEED_ARMS_FACTORY_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_SUPPLY_CONSUMPTION_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PLANNING_SPEED = 0 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM }

		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
		
		# Only refresh party 22's stored leader while the military party is outside government.
		# If it is already ruling or a coalition partner, preserve its incumbent leader.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = gov_coalition_array value = 22 } }
			}
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_CAPSAT_PARTY_LEADER = yes
			set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
		}
		# Legacy-spirit cleanup: these bonuses now live in MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM.
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS }
		MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_BASE = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_COALITION_DEAL }
			MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS = yes
		}

		}
		else = {
			log = "Command already decided as CAPSAT; no command effects reapplied."
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_command.1.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT }
				}
				has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
					has_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
					has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Command settlement - General Staff leadership"
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM_ACTIVATED_TT
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED } }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_COMMAND_POWER_GAIN_MULT = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_TERRAIN_PENALTY_REDUCTION = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ATTACK_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_ORG_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_TRAINING_TIME_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_FOREIGN_INFLUENCE_DEFENSE_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_INTEL_TO_OTHERS = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_REQUIRED_GARRISON_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_LAND_REINFORCE_RATE = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ROOT_OUT_RESISTANCE_EFFECTIVENESS_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_SPECIAL_FORCES_CAP_FLAT = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_ARMY_CORE_DEFENCE_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_EXPERIENCE_GAIN_ARMY_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PERSONNEL_COST_MULTIPLIER_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_MILITARY_SPENDING_COST_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_RESEARCH_SPEED_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_MILITARY_LEADER_COST_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_EQUIPMENT_COST_MULTIPLIER_MODIFIER = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PRODUCTION_SPEED_ARMS_FACTORY_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_SUPPLY_CONSUMPTION_FACTOR = 0 }
			set_variable = { MAD_ARMY_CMD_PLANNING_SPEED = 0 }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM }

		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
		clr_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		# Store the General Staff leader for party 22 only while it is outside government.
		# Do not replace its incumbent leader while it rules or participates in the coalition.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = gov_coalition_array value = 22 } }
			}
			set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			MAD_SELECT_GENERAL_STAFF_PARTY_LEADER = yes
			set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
		}
		
		# Legacy-spirit cleanup: these bonuses now live in MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM.
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS }
		MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_GENERAL_STAFF_BASE = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_COALITION_DEAL }
			MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS = yes
		}

		}
		else = {
			log = "Command already decided as General Staff; no command effects reapplied."
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_command.5
	title = MAD_military_command.5.t
	desc = MAD_military_command.5.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION
		MAD_TGV_IN_POWER = yes
		has_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				has_completed_focus = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_PRIMACY
				has_completed_focus = MAD_ARMY_CENTRALISED_PROCUREMENT
				has_completed_focus = MAD_ARMY_DOCTRINE_ABOVE_FACTION
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED }
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_command.5.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Crush the Mutiny"
		# Noël leads the CAPSAT breakaway; remove his field-command character before the split.
		if = { limit = { has_character = MAD_noel_rakotonandrasana } retire_character = MAD_noel_rakotonandrasana }
		start_civil_war = {
			ideology = nationalist
			ruling_party = nationalist
			size = 0.45
			army_ratio = 0.55
			navy_ratio = 0
			air_ratio = 0
			capital = 267
			states = { 267 487 }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }

			# The breakaway is the CAPSAT / military party, not a generic nationalist revolt.
			set_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
			set_variable = { party_pop_array^22 = 1 }
			set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
			set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_CAPSAT
			clr_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_GENERAL_STAFF
			set_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
			set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
			clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION
			clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
			clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_SELECTED
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_BENI
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_EDMOND
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_ANDRE_LUCIEN
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_PETERA
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_JULES
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_SELECTED
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_MICHAEL
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_LYLISON
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_CHARLES
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_ANDRE_LUCIEN
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_PETERA
			clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_JULES
			set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_SELECTED
			set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_CAPSAT_NOEL
			set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }

			# Convert the copied General Staff baseline to CAPSAT's command baseline.
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND }
				remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_GENERAL_STAFF_COMMAND
				MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_BASE = yes
			}
			else = {
				MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_REMOVE_GENERAL_STAFF_BASE = yes
				MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_BASE = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_COMMAND }
			if = { limit = { has_idea = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS } remove_ideas = MAD_ARMY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Noël Girardin Rakotonandrasana"
				picture = "Noel_Girardin_Rakotonandrasana.dds"
				expire = "2099.1.1"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = { nationalist_Nat_Autocracy military_career }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF_SELECTED } }
			MAD_SELECT_GENERAL_STAFF_PARTY_LEADER = yes
			set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
		}
		
		add_stability = -0.08
		army_experience = 35
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_command.5.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Give CAPSAT a Place in the Settlement"
		# The political concession adds CAPSAT's agreed influence without changing
		# the already selected General Staff / CAPSAT command baseline.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_COALITION_DEAL } }
			MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_APPLY_CAPSAT_CONCESSIONS = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF

		# Add party 22 as a junior coalition partner. This does not replace the
		# incumbent party leader, ruling party or national leader.
		set_temp_variable = { add_col_one = 22 }
		add_coalition_members_effect = yes

		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_COALITION_DEAL
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION

		add_stability = -0.04
		army_experience = 20
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_command.5.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Transfer the Government to the FPA"
		# This outcome transfers political government to the FPA. It deliberately
		# preserves the command choice already made by the General Staff / CAPSAT event.
		set_country_flag = MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_DECIDED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_CAPSAT
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_COMMAND_FORCE_GENERAL_STAFF
		set_country_flag = MAD_CAPSAT_REVOLT_RESOLVED
		clr_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REJECTION

		clr_country_flag = MAD_MIL_PARTY_LEADER_GENERAL_STAFF
		MAD_SELECT_CAPSAT_PARTY_LEADER = yes

		# This is an explicit transfer of the ruling government to party 22.
		# The existing MAD party helper selects its political leader; the current
		# military command flags and MAD_ARMY_COMMAND_SYSTEM remain unchanged.
		set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		MAD_RESET_PARTY_LEADER_COUNTER = yes
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes

		# Fallback for saves where the generic party leader counter prevented
		# creation of the selected FPA leader during the government transfer.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			MAD_CREATE_CAPSAT_PARTY_LEADER = yes
			set_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_MILITARY_RULE_PATH
		add_stability = -0.12
		add_war_support = 0.05
	}
}

# Deferred scheduler for the older TGV/CAPSAT confrontation.
# The actual .5 event and its player choices remain unchanged. The wrapper
# waits for the modern pressure/crisis sequence to conclude before releasing it.
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_command.6
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_TIMER_CANCELLED
					has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_STARTED
					has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED
				}
			}
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Legacy CAPSAT timer cancelled by the modern protest crisis; no confrontation event released"
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = MAD
					date > 2023.12.31
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_PROTEST_CATALYST_FAILED }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_COMPLETED }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_MODERN_CRISIS_STARTED }
				}
				set_country_flag = MAD_TGV_CAPSAT_REVOLT_DEFERRED
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Legacy CAPSAT event deferred pending the modern protest crisis outcome"
			}
			else = {
				country_event = { id = MAD_military_command.5 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- historical service rifle choice ---
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_procurement.1
	title = MAD_military_procurement.1.t
	desc = MAD_military_procurement.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.1.a
		trigger = { country_exists = SAF }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.30 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_3 amount = 6000 producer = SAF } # R4
		SAF = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.30 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAF modifier = military_cooperation }
		army_experience = 15
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.1.b
		trigger = { country_exists = BEL }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.25 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_3 amount = 5000 producer = BEL } # FN FNC
		BEL = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.25 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = BEL modifier = military_cooperation }
		army_experience = 10
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.1.c
		trigger = { country_exists = FRA }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.70 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_4 amount = 5600 producer = FRA } # FAMAS
		FRA = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.70 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = military_cooperation }
		army_experience = 15
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = infantry_weapons_type }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 10
				folder = equipment
				track = infantry_weapons_type
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
	}
}

# --- historical motorisation choice ---
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_procurement.2
	title = MAD_military_procurement.2.t
	desc = MAD_military_procurement.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.2.a
		trigger = { country_exists = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.35 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = util_vehicle_1 amount = 1200 producer = SOV } # GAZ-66
		SOV = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.35 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = military_cooperation }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = motorized_equipment }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = equipment
				track = motorized_equipment
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
		army_experience = 15
	}
	option = {

			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = equipment
				track = motorized_equipment
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
		army_experience = 15
	}
	option = {

			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = military_cooperation }

# --- Indian Ocean field exercise ---
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_procurement.3
	title = MAD_military_procurement.3.t
	desc = MAD_military_procurement.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.3.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: French-led interoperability exercise"
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operational_theory }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = land
				track = operational_theory
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_french_land_fallback
				category = CAT_command_and_control_equipment
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 25
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_french_naval_fallback
				category = CAT_naval_modules
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = training_and_ops }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 25
				folder = air
				track = training_and_ops
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_french_air_fallback
				category = CAT_aircraft
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		army_experience = 20
		navy_experience = 20
		air_experience = 25
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = large_commercial_relations }
			FRA = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = large_commercial_relations }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.3.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: IOC multinational doctrine exercise"
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operational_theory }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = land
				track = operational_theory
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_ioc_land_fallback
				category = CAT_electronics
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_ioc_naval_fallback
				category = CAT_naval_radar
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = training_and_ops }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = air
				track = training_and_ops
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_ioc_air_fallback
				category = CAT_heavy_aircraft
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		army_experience = 25
		navy_experience = 20
		air_experience = 20
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			FRA = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = COM }
			COM = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = COM modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = MRT }
			MRT = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = MRT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SEY }
			SEY = {
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = SEY modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.3.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Malagasy field doctrine"
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operational_theory }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 30
				folder = land
				track = operational_theory
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_malagasy_land_fallback
				category = CAT_infantry
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 10
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_malagasy_naval_fallback
				category = CAT_patrol_boats
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = training_and_ops }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 10
				folder = air
				track = training_and_ops
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = MAD_military_procurement_3_malagasy_air_fallback
				category = CAT_helicopter_operators
				uses = 1
				bonus = 0.15
			}
		}
		army_experience = 30
		navy_experience = 10
		air_experience = 10

		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = officer_international_education }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = officer_international_education
				add_idea = officer_military_academy
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
		change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes

	}
}

# --- historical protected mobility tender ---
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_procurement.5
	title = MAD_military_procurement.5.t
	desc = MAD_military_procurement.5.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.5.a
		trigger = { country_exists = UAE }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.80 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = medium_tank_amphibious_chassis_0 amount = 280 producer = FRA variant_name = "M3 Panhard" }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = APC_1 amount = 280 producer = FRA }
		}
		UAE = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.80 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = UAE modifier = military_cooperation }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_military_procurement_3_panthera category = CAT_armored_fighting_vehicles category = CAT_armored_personnel_carriers uses = 1 bonus = 0.25 }
		army_experience = 15
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.5.b
		trigger = { country_exists = SAF }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = medium_tank_flame_chassis_1 amount = 175 producer = SAF variant_name = "Ratel-20" }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = APC_3 amount = 175 producer = SAF }
		}
		SAF = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAF modifier = military_cooperation }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = motorized_equipment }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = equipment
				track = motorized_equipment
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
		army_experience = 15
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.5.c
		trigger = { country_exists = TUR }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.90 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = medium_tank_amphibious_chassis_1 amount = 120 producer = TUR variant_name = "ACV-15 AAPC" }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = APC_2 amount = 120 producer = TUR }
		}
		TUR = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.90 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = military_cooperation }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = motorized_equipment }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = equipment
				track = motorized_equipment
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
		army_experience = 15
	}
}

# --- historical air package expansion ---# --- historical air package expansion ---
country_event = {
	id = MAD_military_procurement.4
	title = MAD_military_procurement.4.t
	desc = MAD_military_procurement.4.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.4.a
		trigger = { country_exists = SAF }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.90 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_helicopter_1 amount = 60 producer = SAF } } # Atlas Oryx
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_helicopter_2 amount = 60 producer = SAF } } # Atlas Oryx
		SAF = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.90 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAF modifier = military_cooperation }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = training_and_ops }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = air
				track = training_and_ops
			}
		}
		else = {
			air_experience = 25
		}
		air_experience = 25
		army_experience = 10
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.4.b
		trigger = { country_exists = FRA }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = large_plane_air_transport_airframe_2 amount = 20 producer = SPR variant_name = "Airbus C-295" }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane3 amount = 20 producer = SPR }
		}
		FRA = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1.50 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 8 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_france_military_cooperation_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_madagascar_military_cooperation_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			FRA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = heavy_aircraft }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = air
				track = heavy_aircraft
			}
		}
		else = {
			air_experience = 25
		}
		air_experience = 30
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_procurement.4.c
		trigger = { country_exists = SAF country_exists = FRA }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.90 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_helicopter_1 amount = 60 producer = SAF } } # Atlas Oryx
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_helicopter_2 amount = 60 producer = SAF } } # Atlas Oryx
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.75 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = large_plane_air_transport_airframe_2 amount = 10 producer = SPR variant_name = "Airbus C-295" }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane3 amount = 10 producer = SPR }
		}
		SAF = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.90 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		FRA = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.75 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAF modifier = military_cooperation }
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_france_military_cooperation_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_madagascar_military_cooperation_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			FRA = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = training_and_ops }

			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }

		}
		army_experience = 25
	}

		SAF = {

			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = military_cooperation }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAF modifier = military_cooperation }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = motorized_equipment }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 20
				folder = equipment
				track = motorized_equipment
			}
		}
		else = {
			army_experience = 25
		}
		army_experience = 20
	}

add_namespace = mad_roy_rework

# Ndriana branch - MAD_POL_ROY_BIBLICAL_GEOPOLITICS
country_event = {
	id = mad_roy_rework.1
	title = mad_roy_rework.1.t
	desc = mad_roy_rework.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	# A. Formal covenant partnership with Israel
	option = {
		name = mad_roy_rework.1.a
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event mad_roy_rework.1 option a"

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ISR }
			ISR = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.00 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
				set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 5 }
				modify_international_investment_effect = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = MAD modifier = large_commercial_relations }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -3.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			# Preserve the old diplomatic choice's mutual declaration of loyalty.
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ISR modifier = has_expressed_loyalty }
			ISR = {
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = MAD modifier = has_expressed_loyalty }
			}
			# Israel gains influence in Madagascar under the new covenant.
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ISR }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			# The legacy diplomatic outcome also increased Madagascar's influence in Israel.
			# Reset tag_index explicitly so the previous ISR -> MAD adjustment cannot leak into this one.
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ISR.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		# The old diplomatic choice also granted war support, independently of Israel's presence.
		add_war_support = 0.02
		one_random_agriculture_district = yes
		add_named_threat = { threat = 2 name = "Madagascar Aligns with Israel" }
		set_country_flag = MAD_ROY_ISRAEL_COVENANT
	}

	# B. Church, pilgrimage and agricultural ties only
	option = {
		name = mad_roy_rework.1.b
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event mad_roy_rework.1 option b"

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ISR }
			ISR = {
				set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 5 }
				modify_international_investment_effect = yes
			}
		}
		one_random_agriculture_district = yes
		set_country_flag = MAD_ROY_ISRAEL_PILGRIM_TIES
	}

	# C. Blessing to all nations - no alignment
	option = {
		name = mad_roy_rework.1.c
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event mad_roy_rework.1 option c"

		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
		}
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = MAD_ROY_BIBLICAL_SCHOLARSHIP
			category = CAT_civilian
			bonus = 0.10
			uses = 1
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_ROY_ISRAEL_NEUTRAL
	}
}

# Royal succession crisis: a delayed challenge to Ndriana's lineage after the King's Mandate.
country_event = {
	id = mad_royal.6
	title = mad_royal.6.t
	desc = mad_royal.6.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.6.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.6.a - succession protests suppressed"
		add_stability = -0.08
		increase_policing_budget = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.08 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.6.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.6.b - succession protests permitted"
		add_stability = -0.04
		267 = {
			# Damage approximately half of the existing building level capacity; the damage can be repaired later.
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_infrastructure_damage = building_level@infrastructure }
			multiply_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_infrastructure_damage = 0.5 }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_tana_infrastructure_damage > 0 } infrastructure > 0 }
				damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 0.5 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_infrastructure_damage = 0.5 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_offices_damage = building_level@offices }
			multiply_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_offices_damage = 0.5 }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { MAD_tana_offices_damage > 0 } offices > 0 }
				damage_building = { type = offices damage = 0.5 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { MAD_tana_offices_damage = 0.5 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

# France receives separate sovereignty demands. Reunion and Mayotte are resolved independently.
country_event = {
	id = mad_royal.10
	title = mad_royal.10.t
	desc = mad_royal.10.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.10.a - French government transfers Reunion"
		if = {
			limit = { 706 = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			706 = { add_core_of = MAD transfer_state_to = MAD }
		}
		MAD = {
			add_stability = 0.01
			add_war_support = 0.02
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { MAD = { num_divisions > 25 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { MAD = { has_navy_size = { size > 10 } } } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 99 } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_government = MAD
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.10.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.10.b - French government refuses Reunion demand"
		MAD = {
			add_war_support = 0.03
			add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = MAD_ROY_FRANCE_REFUSES_REUNION_TENSION }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 706 = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			MAD = { add_state_claim = 706 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_diplomacy.91 days = 1 } }
	}
}

	id = mad_royal.11
	title = mad_royal.11.t
	desc = mad_royal.11.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.11.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.11.a - French government transfers Mayotte"
		# Transfer French-owned states belonging to Mayotte's MTE core, if any.
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MTE }
			add_core_of = MAD
			transfer_state_to = MAD
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = MTE }
			MTE = { every_owned_state = { add_core_of = MAD } }
			MAD = { annex_country = { target = MTE } }
		}
		MAD = {
			add_stability = 0.01
			add_war_support = 0.02
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { MAD = { num_divisions > 25 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { MAD = { has_navy_size = { size > 10 } } } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 99 } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_government = MAD
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_royal.11.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_royal.11.b - French government refuses Mayotte demand"
		MAD = {
			add_war_support = 0.03
			add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = MAD_ROY_FRANCE_REFUSES_MAYOTTE_TENSION }
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MTE }
			MAD = { add_state_claim = PREV }
		}

			limit = { country_exists = MTE }

			every_owned_state = {

			}
		}

	}

# Event 1 — Chapter I: the first institutional review.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.1
	title = mad_naval.1.t
	desc = mad_naval.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.1.a executed"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_PRIORITY_COASTAL
		# Two radar levels at Antsiranana: $3.5bn total.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -3.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = radar_station
				level = 2
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			navy_experience = 10
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_REVIEW_COASTAL category = CAT_naval_radar bonus = 0.15 uses = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" }
			mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 500 add_mio_research_bonus = 0.02 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.1.b executed"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_PRIORITY_OFFSHORE
		# Expand the SECREN harbour at Antsiranana province 5222 by two port levels: $1bn.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = naval_base
				level = 2
				province = 5222
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = escort_ships }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = naval
				track = escort_ships
			}
		}
		else = {
			navy_experience = 10
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_REVIEW_OFFSHORE category = CAT_corvettes bonus = 0.15 uses = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" }
			mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 500 add_mio_research_bonus = 0.02 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.1.c executed"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_PRIORITY_SECREN
		# Establish one SECREN dockyard at Antsiranana: $7.5bn.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -7.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" } mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 200 add_mio_research_bonus = 0.02 } }
	}
}

# Event 2 — Chapter II: select the first practical modernisation emphasis.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.2
	title = mad_naval.2.t
	desc = mad_naval.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.2.a executed"
		# Fortify the four harbour provinces — Antsiranana, Mahajanga, Toliara and Toamasina: $0.5bn each, $2bn total.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = { add_building_construction = { type = coastal_bunker level = 1 province = 5222 instant_build = yes } } # Antsiranana
		266 = { add_building_construction = { type = coastal_bunker level = 1 province = 8188 instant_build = yes } } # Mahajanga
		268 = { add_building_construction = { type = coastal_bunker level = 1 province = 12956 instant_build = yes } } # Toliara
		487 = { add_building_construction = { type = coastal_bunker level = 1 province = 615 instant_build = yes } } # Toamasina
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			navy_experience = 10
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" }

		}

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.2.c executed"
		# Add one naval-base level at the SECREN port, Antsiranana province 5222: $0.5bn.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = { add_building_construction = { type = naval_base level = 1 province = 5222 instant_build = yes } }

		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_FAST_INTERCEPTION category = CAT_patrol_boats bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_FAST_INTERCEPTION category = CAT_landing_craft bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = operations }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 10
				folder = naval
				track = operations
			}
		}
		else = {
			navy_experience = 5
		}
	}
}

# Event 3 — Chapter III: the industrial route to a locally sustained corvette.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.3
	title = mad_naval.3.t
	desc = mad_naval.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.3.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.3.a executed"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_DESIGN_LICENSED
		# Prefer France's latest corvette design. If France is unavailable,
		# at war, or has no corvettes in service, fall back to South Korea.
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = FRA
				FRA = {
					NOT = { has_war = yes }
					has_tech = corvette_hull_3
					has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
				}
			}
			FRA = {
				create_production_license = {
					target = MAD
					new_prioritised = yes
					equipment = { type = corvette_hull_3 }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = KOR
					KOR = {
						NOT = { has_war = yes }
						has_tech = corvette_hull_2
						has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
					}
				}
				KOR = {
					create_production_license = {
						target = MAD
						new_prioritised = yes
						equipment = { type = corvette_hull_2 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Send the joint-development proposal to the first eligible partner.
	# The recipient is shown directly in the option; a refusal never triggers
	# another offer. If neither partner is eligible, retain the domestic route.
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.3.b_fra
		trigger = {
			country_exists = FRA
			FRA = {
				NOT = { has_war = yes }
				has_tech = corvette_hull_3
				has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.3.b_fra executed (France)"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_DESIGN_JOINT
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_JOINT_CORVETTE category = CAT_corvettes bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" } mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 400 } }
		FRA = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.10 days = 1 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.3.b_kor
		trigger = {
			country_exists = KOR
			KOR = {
				NOT = { has_war = yes }
				has_tech = corvette_hull_2
				has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
			}
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					country_exists = FRA
					FRA = {
						NOT = { has_war = yes }
						has_tech = corvette_hull_3
						has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.3.b_kor executed (South Korea)"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_DESIGN_JOINT
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_JOINT_CORVETTE category = CAT_corvettes bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" } mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 400 } }
		KOR = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.10 days = 1 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.3.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						country_exists = FRA
						FRA = {
							NOT = { has_war = yes }
							has_tech = corvette_hull_3
							has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						country_exists = KOR
						KOR = {
							NOT = { has_war = yes }
							has_tech = corvette_hull_2
							has_navy_size = { size > 0 unit = corvette }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.3.b domestic route (no eligible foreign partner)"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_DESIGN_JOINT
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_JOINT_CORVETTE category = CAT_corvettes bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" } mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 400 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.3.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.3.c executed"
		set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_DESIGN_DOMESTIC
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_DOMESTIC_CORVETTE category = CAT_corvettes bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = MAD_NAVAL_DOMESTIC_CORVETTE category = CAT_naval_modules bonus = 0.50 uses = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" } mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = { add_mio_funds = 1000 add_mio_research_bonus = 0.05 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_subdoctrine_in_track = escort_ships }
			add_mastery = {
				amount = 15
				folder = naval
				track = escort_ships
			}
		}
		else = {
			navy_experience = 10
		}
	}
}

# Event 6 — Optional helicopter-operator concept conclusion. This authorises further work, not a free hull.
country_event = {

	desc = mad_naval.6.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	# The single proposal goes to the first partner available. A refusal does not
	# trigger another proposal; the recipient is identified in this event option.
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.6.a
		trigger = {
			country_exists = FRA
			FRA = { NOT = { has_war = yes } }
		}
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.6.a executed (France)"

				folder = naval

		}

		}

			country_exists = KOR
			KOR = { NOT = { has_war = yes } }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					country_exists = FRA
					FRA = { NOT = { has_war = yes } }
				}
			}
		}

		KOR = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.12 days = 1 } }

# Event 7 — France decides whether to sell Madagascar two 1975-era corvettes.
# Total purchase price is $700m ($350m per corvette): Madagascar pays it and France receives the same amount.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.7
	title = mad_naval.7.t
	desc = mad_naval.7.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_naval.7.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.7.a accepted French corvette sale"

		# Clear any stale fallback flag from an earlier attempt.
		clr_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_RECREATE_65_CORVETTES

		# Check whether France has at least two corvettes.
		# Prefer the two named Tripartite-class ships.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_navy_size = {
					size > 1
					unit = corvette
				}
			}

			transfer_ship = {
				prefer_name = "Pégase (M644)"
				type = corvette
				target = MAD
				exclude_refitting = no
			}

			transfer_ship = {
				prefer_name = "Orion (M645)"
				type = corvette
				target = MAD
				exclude_refitting = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Fallback: remove available French corvettes and
			# create the replacement ships for Madagascar in Event 8.
			destroy_ships = {
				type = corvette
				count = 2
			}

			set_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_RECREATE_65_CORVETTES
		}

		# France receives the sale payment.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.70 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Increase French influence over Madagascar.
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		change_influence_percentage = yes

		# Trigger Madagascar's receipt event.
		MAD = {
			country_event = {
				id = mad_naval.8
				days = 1
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 6
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_naval.7.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.7.b declined French corvette sale"

		MAD = {
			country_event = {
				id = mad_naval.9
				days = 1
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 4
		}
	}
}

# Event 8 — Madagascar is notified that the French corvettes have been purchased.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.8
	title = mad_naval.8.t
	desc = mad_naval.8.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_naval.8.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.8.a corvettes received"

		# Fallback only: create replacement ships if Event 7
		# could not use its corvette-transfer branch.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					has_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_RECREATE_65_CORVETTES
				}
			}

			create_ship = {
				type = corvette_hull_1
				equipment_variant = "Tripartite Class"
				creator = FRA
				name = "Pégase (M644)"
			}

			create_ship = {
				type = corvette_hull_1
				equipment_variant = "Tripartite Class"
				creator = FRA
				name = "Orion (M645)"
			}

			FRA = {
				clr_country_flag = MAD_NAVAL_RECREATE_65_CORVETTES
			}
		}

		# Madagascar pays for the purchase.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.70 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes


		# Fully complete Naval Vessel Experimentation
		# only if it has not already been completed.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_special_project_completed = sp:sp_naval_vessel_project
				}
			}

			complete_special_project = sp:sp_naval_vessel_project
		}

		# Grant the 1965 Corvette technology only if missing.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_tech = corvette_hull_1
				}
			}

			set_technology = {
				corvette_hull_1 = 1
			}
		}
	}
}
# Event 9 — France declines the request; no money or ships change hands.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.9
	title = mad_naval.9.t
	desc = mad_naval.9.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_naval.9.a French sale declined"
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = MAD_offer_refused }
		}
	}
}

# Event 10 — France, or South Korea as the fallback partner, considers Madagascar's corvette co-development proposal.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.10
	title = mad_naval.10.t
	desc = mad_naval.10.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: accepted Madagascar's corvette co-development proposal"
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -7.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 10.00 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = FRA }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { tag_index = KOR } }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = MAD modifier = MAD_corvette_cooperation }
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.11 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { add = 40 check_variable = { treasury > 7.499 } }
			modifier = { add = 20 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 0 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.10.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: declined Madagascar's corvette co-development proposal"
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.14 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Event 11 — Madagascar receives the accepting partner's decision and funds its share.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.11
	title = mad_naval.11.t
	desc = mad_naval.11.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = mad_naval.11.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: corvette co-development agreement accepted by [FROM.GetName]"
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = MAD_corvette_cooperation }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		266 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Event 12 — A foreign partner considers Madagascar's helicopter-operator programme.
country_event = {
	id = mad_naval.12
	title = mad_naval.12.t
	desc = mad_naval.12.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_political
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_naval.12.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: accepted Madagascar's helicopter-operator partnership proposal"
		# The partner commits $7.5bn, records $10bn in international investment,
		# and receives a 5% increase in influence over Madagascar.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -7.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 10.00 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = FRA }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = KOR }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = KOR }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = CHI }
			}
		}
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.13 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { add = 40 check_variable = { treasury > 7.499 } }
			modifier = { add = 20 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 0 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_naval.12.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: declined Madagascar's helicopter-operator partnership proposal"
		MAD = { country_event = { id = mad_naval.15 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Rural Cooperation — Southern Services Compact outcome event.
add_namespace = mad_rural_cooperation

# Southern Services Compact — the programme's delivery contract is chosen only after
# 180 days of mobilising the logistics, village institutions and security presence.

country_event = {
	id = mad_rural_cooperation.1
	title = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_T
	desc = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_D
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_A
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_A_desc
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_ROY_ANTENIMIERAN_ANDRIANA
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_ROY_TAMIMA_REPRESENT_LIVING_FORCES
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_ROY_DINA_CUSTOMARY_JUSTICE
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_AREMA_FOKONOLONA
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_AREMA_FOOD_FIRST
					has_completed_focus = MAD_POL_AREMA_PROVINCES
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 15 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 12 } }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		268 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = -12 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
		}
		MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_political_power = 15
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_B
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_B_desc
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 8 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_DAHALO_DEFEATED }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MAD_REMENABILA_IN_POWER }
			}
			add_to_variable = { MAD_dahalo_threat = -10 tooltip = MAD_DAHALO_THREAT_MINUS_10_TT }
			MAD_dahalo_update_modifier = yes
		}
		268 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = -8 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
		}
		MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
		add_stability = 0.02
		army_experience = 5
	}

	option = {
		name = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_C
		custom_effect_tooltip = MAD_RURAL_SERVICES_EVENT_C_desc
		set_temp_variable = { MAD_rc_delta = 10 }
		MAD_rural_cooperation_adjust = yes
		268 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = -8 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
			if = {
				limit = { can_construct_building = infrastructure }
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
		}
		266 = {
			set_temp_variable = { MAD_dr_state_famine = -8 }
			MAD_drought_state_apply_famine = yes
		}
		MAD_drought_recalc_national = yes
		add_stability = 0.01
	}
}

add_namespace = mad_secren

# France decides whether to finance the SECREN modernisation programme.
country_event = {
	id = mad_secren.1
	title = mad_secren.1.t
	desc = mad_secren.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_secren.1.a
		trigger = {
			MAD = {
				has_country_flag = MAD_SECREN_FRENCH_INVESTMENT_REQUESTED
				266 = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = MAD
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { add = 60 check_variable = { treasury > 7.499 } }
			modifier = { add = 10 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 20 } }
			modifier = { add = 30 has_opinion = { target = MAD value > 79 } }
		}

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: French SECREN investment accepted"

		# France pays $7.5 billion.
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -7.50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Record $7.5 billion in French international investments.
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 7.50 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes

		# Increase French influence in Madagascar by 5%.
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = MAD }
		change_influence_percentage = yes

		# Madagascar receives the investment and executes the project.
		MAD = {
			country_event = {
				id = mad_secren.2
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_secren.1.b
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: French SECREN investment declined"

		MAD = {
			country_event = {
				id = mad_secren.3
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_secren.2
	title = mad_secren.2.t
	desc = mad_secren.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_secren.2.a

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: French investment in SECREN completed"


		# The investment goes directly to SECREN, not Madagascar's treasury.
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny" }
			mio:MAD_SECREN_shipbuilding = {
				add_mio_funds = 3000
			}
		}

		# Expand the shipyard at Antsiranana.
		266 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1

			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_secren.3
	title = mad_secren.3.t
	desc = mad_secren.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_secren.3.a

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: France declined SECREN investment"

		set_country_flag = MAD_SECREN_FRENCH_INVESTMENT_DECLINED
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = MAD_offer_refused }
	}
}

			base = 20

			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = COM.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		if = {

		}

country_event = {
	id = mad_diplomacy.13
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_mrt_ioc_accepted
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_com_ioc_accepted
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_mrt_ioc_accepted
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_sey_ioc_accepted
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_com_ioc_accepted
						has_country_flag = MAD_dip_sey_ioc_accepted
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_network }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_network
					add_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_active
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_active } }
				add_ideas = MAD_dip_ioc_active
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_network }
					NOT = { has_idea = MAD_dip_ioc_active }
				}
				add_ideas = MAD_dip_ioc_network
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = mad_diplomacy.20
	title = mad_diplomacy.20.t
	desc = mad_diplomacy.20.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_diplomacy.20.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_diplomacy.20.a executed"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = { add = 25 has_opinion = { target = FROM value > 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { NOT = { check_variable = { treasury > 0.75 } } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse } }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = improve_trade }
		set_temp_variable = { receiver_nation = ROOT.id }
		set_temp_variable = { sender_nation = FROM.id }
		set_improved_trade_agreement = yes
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.75 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = improve_trade }
			add_ideas = MAD_dip_mauritius_trade_spirit
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
				change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
				change_international_bankers_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = farmers }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
				change_farmers_opinion = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_diplomacy.20.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_diplomacy.20.b executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 35 }
		FROM = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = diplomatic_distance }
			country_event = { id = mad_diplomacy.21 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

	id = mad_diplomacy.51
	title = mad_diplomacy.51.t

	picture = GFX_report_event_economy_default

	option = {
		name = mad_diplomacy.51.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_diplomacy.51.a executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 55
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { ai_has_high_deficit = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { check_variable = { treasury > 2.00 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { NOT = { check_variable = { treasury > 4.999 } } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		487 = {
			if = {
				limit = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size < 1 include_locked = no } }
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 5.00 }
			modify_international_investment_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = MAD_dip_china_local_content days = 1825 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		}

			limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_industrial_conglomerates_opinion = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = labour_unions }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
			change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_diplomacy.51.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: mad_diplomacy.51.b executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_government = democratic }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { ai_has_high_deficit = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { check_variable = { treasury > 1.00 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { NOT = { check_variable = { treasury > 2.999 } } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -3.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 3.00 }
			modify_international_investment_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = THIS.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}

			category = CAT_industry
			uses = 2
			bonus = 0.15
		}

			category = CAT_electronics
			uses = 2
			bonus = 0.15
		}
