			limit = { has_idea = labour_unions }

			change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_small_medium_business_owners_opinion = yes
		}
	}

		add_political_power = 10

# ============================================================================
# 2017 Madagascar pneumonic plague emergency
# The domestic chain concludes by unlocking the permanent surveillance decision.
# ============================================================================
country_event = {
	id = mad_plague.1
	title = mad_plague.1.t
	desc = mad_plague.1.d
	picture = GFX_african_school
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = mad_plague.1.a # Mobilise the national health services
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_plague.1.a - full national response"
		set_temp_variable = {
			treasury_change = {
				value = MAD.gdp_total
				multiply = -0.005 # Emergency response costs 0.5% of Madagascar GDP.
			}
		}
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_stability = -0.02
		decrease_economic_growth = yes
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_EMERGENCY
			days = 90
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_FULL_RESPONSE
		set_global_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_OUTBREAK
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = { id = mad_plague.2 days = 30 random_days = 10 }
			country_event = { id = mad_plague.3 days = 74 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_plague.1.b # Concentrate resources on affected cities
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: mad_plague.1.b - targeted response"
		add_stability = -0.05
		decrease_economic_growth = yes
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_EMERGENCY
			days = 150
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_TARGETED_RESPONSE
		set_global_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_OUTBREAK
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = { id = mad_plague.2 days = 30 random_days = 10 }
			country_event = { id = mad_plague.3 days = 74 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

# Late-November containment unlocks the existing National Epidemic Surveillance Charter.
country_event = {
	id = mad_plague.3
	title = mad_plague.3.t
	desc = mad_plague.3.d
	picture = GFX_african_school
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_FULL_RESPONSE
			has_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_TARGETED_RESPONSE
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = mad_plague.3.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_FULL_RESPONSE }
			add_stability = 0.04
		}
		else = {
			add_stability = 0.02
		}
		set_country_flag = MAD_2017_PLAGUE_CONTAINED
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: 2017 pneumonic plague outbreak contained; surveillance charter unlocked"
	}
}

	fire_only_once = yes

		name = MAD_military_refoundation.17.a

		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event MAD_military_refoundation.17.a - retain the State Secretariat"
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_MIL_PRE2002_GENDARMERIE_SECRETARIAT
			days = 1095
		}
	}

	# Bring forward the National Gendarmerie Command established in 2002.
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_refoundation.17.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event MAD_military_refoundation.17.b - establish a National Gendarmerie Command"
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_MIL_PRE2002_GENDARMERIE_COMMAND
			days = 1095
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = the_military }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
	}

	# Extend the 1990s mixed-command approach into civilian oversight.
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_refoundation.17.c
		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event MAD_military_refoundation.17.c - establish civilian security coordination"
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = MAD_MIL_PRE2002_GENDARMERIE_CIVILIAN_COORDINATION
			days = 1095
		}
		add_stability = -0.01
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = the_military }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
	}
}

		name = MAD_military_refoundation.10.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 75 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Negotiate November 2010 military challenge"
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_2010_MUTINY_NEGOTIATED
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_political_power = 15
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.01 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = MAD_military_refoundation.11 days = 300 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_refoundation.10.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Suppress November 2010 military challenge"
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_2010_MUTINY_SUPPRESSED
		add_stability = -0.04
		army_experience = 10
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.03 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = the_military }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -10 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = MAD_military_refoundation.11 days = 300 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = MAD_military_refoundation.10.c
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Formalise military mediation role"
		set_country_flag = MAD_MIL_2010_TRANSITIONAL_GUARANTOR
		add_timed_idea = { idea = MAD_MIL_HAT_TRANSITION_GUARANTOR days = 1095 }
		add_stability = -0.02
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 22 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.05 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.05 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = MAD_military_refoundation.11 days = 300 } }
	}
}
